--- Page 180 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Year Lord's placement. The previous page concluded by noting that if the Year Lord is in a human sign in the difficult sixth house, servants are lost. This page begins with the results for "four-legged" signs in the eighth and twelfth houses before moving to the effects of planets in the auspicious tenth house.</meta>

# Results of the Year Lord in Difficult and Auspicious Houses

If the planet is situated in a **four-legged sign** <note>original: chatushpada-rashi; these include Aries, Taurus, Leo, and parts of Sagittarius and Capricorn</note>, even if it is located in the **eighth house** <note>the house of obstacles and longevity</note> or the **twelfth house** <note>the house of loss and expenditure</note>, there will be quarrels and conflict with servants.

Now, regarding **Saturn** <note>original: Manda</note> as the <term>Year Lord: the planet that rules the chart for a specific year in the Tajika system</term>: if he is **devoid of strength** and situated in the sixth or twelfth house, there will be "residing on the land." This refers to the construction of houses or villages, or the development of that land through the planting of trees and the digging of wells and water-tanks.

### Results of Planets in the Tenth House

Now, the author speaks of the results of the planets when they are situated in other positions, specifically the **tenth house** <note>the house of career, status, and actions</note>.

**Saturn in its own sign or exaltation:** If Saturn (the "Son of the Sun") is the Year Lord and is in his own signs <note>Capricorn or Aquarius</note> or his sign of exaltation <note>Libra</note>, and is located in the tenth house, there will be freedom from disease <note>original: nairujyam</note> in the body and the acquisition of wealth.

**Jupiter:** If **Jupiter** <note>original: Jiva or Guru</note> is similarly the Year Lord, situated in Sagittarius, Pisces, or Cancer, and is in the tenth house, health and the acquisition of wealth will occur.

**The Sun:** If the **Sun** is similarly situated in Leo or Aries in the tenth house, wealth is obtained from the King <note>representing the government or high authorities</note>.

->**When in their own or exalted signs in the tenth house: from Saturn comes health and wealth; so too from Jupiter. From the Sun, wealth comes from the King; from Mars, through the strength of one's own arms; from Mercury, through medicine, astrology, poetry, and crafts. (4)**<-
->**If Saturn is the Year Lord and is devoid of strength, predict lack of health and a wretched state. If the Sun is the Year Lord, and Saturn occupies the sign of the Moon in either the birth or annual chart, (5)**<-
->**there is failure in all undertakings; similarly if Mars is eclipsed. [Results are also spoiled if] the tenth house, its ruler, or the Sahamana lords are joined or aspected by Saturn. (6)**<-

**Mars:** If **Mars** <note>original: Kuja or Bhauma</note> is similarly the Year Lord, situated in Aries, Scorpio, or Capricorn in the tenth house, wealth is obtained through the strength of one's own arms <note>implying physical labor, military service, or independent effort</note>.

**Mercury:** If **Mercury** <note>original: Budha</note> is similarly the Year Lord, situated in Gemini or Virgo in the tenth house, wealth is obtained through the practice of **medicine**, **astrology**, **poetry**, or **crafts**.

Now, the author explains the results of a **weak Saturn** in the first half of the fifth verse. If Saturn is the Year Lord and is devoid of strength <note>original: gatabale</note> while in the tenth house, one should predict despair and a restless, unsteady mind.

Next, he explains the results when the **Sun** is the Year Lord using the latter half of the fifth verse and the beginning of the sixth. If the Sun is the Year Lord, and Saturn is occupying the sign where the Moon was located at the time of birth <note>the Janma Rashi</note> or the time of the annual chart, or if these positions are "hurt" <note>afflicted</note> by malefic planets...

<summary>This page details the professional and financial outcomes of different planets serving as the Year Lord in the tenth house, while also warning of the failures that occur when Saturn is weak or afflicting key points in the horoscope.</summary>
<keywords>Year Lord, Abdapa, Tenth House, Saturn, Jupiter, Sun, Mars, Mercury, professional success, health, Tajika astrology</keywords>

--- Page 181 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the strength and placement of planets in the annual horoscope (Varshaphala). It specifically addresses the affliction of the Year Lord and the tenth house of actions before moving into general principles for comparing the birth chart (Janma) with the annual chart (Varsha).</meta>

Page ८९
Tajika Neelakanthi
Section २

When the planet is afflicted, there will be failure in all actions and a lack of knowledge and other virtues. Similarly, if **Saturn** <note>original: Shani</note> is **retrograde** <note>original: vakra; appearing to move backward</note> or **combust** <note>original: astaṅgate; too close to the sun to be visible</note>, there will be failure in all actions. 

Now, the author speaks in the second half of the verse regarding the inauspicious results arising from the conjunction or aspect of Saturn upon the **tenth house** <note>the house of career and public life</note>, the **Lord of the Tenth**, and the **Master of the Saham of Action** <note>original: Karma Sahama; a specific calculated point in Tajika astrology related to one's work</note>.

If the tenth house, its lord, or the master of the Saham of action are **joined or aspected by Saturn**, then failure in all actions will occur. || ५ || ६ || 

Now he explains the results when the **Year Lord** <note>original: Varshalesha or Abdesha; the planet ruling the specific year</note> is placed in a difficult house. 

-> If the **Year Lord** is in the **sixth, eighth, or twelfth house** <note>the three "dusthanas" or houses of difficulty</note>, or if the **Lord of the Tenth** <note>original: Karmesha</note> is devoid of strength in both the birth chart and the annual chart, it is not auspicious. The same applies to the **Lord of the Eighth house** <note>original: mṛtipe; the house of death and transformation</note> during that year. || ७ || <-

-> Whatever house a planet occupies in the **birth chart**—whether it gives good or bad results—that same planet will surely give the same results when it occupies that same house in the **annual chart**. || ८ || <-

-> Those planets that are strong in the birth chart but weak in the annual chart give auspicious results in the first half of the year and inauspicious results in the latter half. The opposite is true if the strengths are reversed. If the strengths are equal, the results are equal in both parts. || ९ || <-

-> This concludes the consideration of the **Twelfth House** <note>original: vyaya-bhāva-vichāraḥ; the house of loss and liberation</note>. <-

If the planet is in the sixth, eighth, or twelfth house and is weak, no good will come of it. || ७ || 

Now he speaks generally regarding the auspicious and inauspicious results for the year. In the birth chart, in whichever house a planet is located—whether it be a giver of auspicious or inauspicious results—that same planet, when situated in that same house in the annual chart, will definitely <note>original: dhruvam</note> give those results. || ८ ||

Now he explains the timing of when these planetary results will manifest <note>original: phala-pāka-kāla</note> during the year. Those planets which were **strong** in the birth chart but are **powerless** <note>original: virvīryā</note> in the annual chart will give happiness in the **first half of the year** <note>original: pūrvārdhe</note> but will give inauspicious results in the **latter half** <note>original: uttarārdhe</note>. 

However, in the opposite case, they give the reverse result. That is to say: those planets that were weak in the birth chart but are strong in the annual chart will give inauspicious results in the first half of the year and happiness in the latter half.

<summary>This page outlines how to interpret the Year Lord's placement in difficult houses and explains a fundamental rule: a planet's behavior in the annual chart mirrors its role in the birth chart, with its strength determining whether good or bad results manifest in the first or second half of the year.</summary>

<keywords>Year Lord, Saturn, Tenth House, Saham of Action, Sixth House, Eighth House, Twelfth House, planetary strength, birth chart vs. annual chart, timing of results</keywords>
